# The decisions

The model owner's decisions in force, one line each, grouped by topic; a new decision replaces the line it changes and a line that no longer holds is deleted. The law is [ALGEBRA.md](ALGEBRA.md) and the engine is [ENGINE.md](ENGINE.md); git keeps the history.

## The law's words

- **The click.** A click is the passage of information from Node to Node, at most one Node per interval, its content an amplitude with phase; reality is the clicks, and Lorentz is the symmetry of the clicks Outside, arrived at from the two assumptions of the click theorem. Why the click: whoever built nature so wanted it known from outside, and locally, that a body is one body; from one click at one place we learn that the whole body functions as one, and that is what the board transmits (the owner, 2026-09-28, 07:08 to 07:11 Israel, to Nature24 directly).
- **The measurement rule.** Only a detector's click is a measurement, compared with nature or pinned; a GameBoard reading (the host's view of levels, remainders and counts) is a diagnostic labelled so; no experiment reads the GameBoard and nobody measures the board; a number whose kind is not named is not a result.
- **Only clicks against clicks (the owner, 2026-09-30, via the advisor, "we compare only clicks with clicks", #1519 comment 5908408109).** Everything nature measures is clicks, click patterns and no protons or electrons behind them, so a finding against nature is a click pattern of the law, from a look, against a click pattern of nature, from an experiment, and nothing else: a GameBoard reading (a radius, a centroid's drift, a level) names a look to design and is never a finding, and a mismatch stated from a board reading leaves the findings. The index of the findings (#1519) is kept by this rule: exclusivity, Q / M, gravitational waves, the charge's unit, the proton against the neutron, the antineutron, the rotation curves and the redshifts, the bending factor and Bell stay as click statements with their looks; light's speed against the causal bound is no finding (the bound is no click of nature's); the hierarchy is the charged cloud look's row; the finding of scale is written only from a scattering look (matter of short wavelength off a cloud onto a screen, the pattern's width against the electron's Compton pattern) and the compact body's fall only from the fall look's rises at detectors. Every blind row the advisor writes is a click pattern from here on.
- **Inside and Outside.** Inside is inside the GameBoard, where no one measures; Outside is the game above the board, the detectors and their clicks, which the paper claims represents reality and shows the match, never that nature is so; a detector's clock is what it emits and receives back, on its own record.
- **The click ends nothing (the owner, 2026-09-30, on the advisor's answer #1515 comment 5903463643, replacing The click, the one non-local act).** The click is the detector's report of the current into its region, the meeting of the future with the past there (**The click is the meeting of the future with the past**), local; the record goes on after it and nothing ends anywhere; the ending of a record at a click (the old line: the record ends at once, everywhere; a detector that takes the quantum into its own count, the absorption) is decided against and is no hypothesis of the law (the owner, 2026-09-30, "yes" on #1515 B and "perhaps nature is local"): the local click is the law's claim, tested against nature by the two slits' dilution series (feeble light and a lone quantum never click at a cold detector Node, every remainder 0, and click at a warm one by its remainder, below, and one quantum clicks once at a resonant absorber); no signalling holds trivially, and the Outside reads the clicks alone. Absorption by a body is the read of the sign holder and the write, no conversion (the owner, 2026-09-30, #1519 comment 5904289939, on the advisor's 5904258920; the sense has since left, **There is no sense**: a body of dimension 2 reads the holder plainly): a body reads the sign holder's level into its paces, a drive at the light's frequency, resonant where it meets the body's rotation, and its write answers, the driven body writing light of its own that cancels the incoming events behind it; where the events are cancelled their current stops, so the light's share that arrived at the body's Nodes does not leave and stands there as the record's share (the count's line has left, **The count's line and the sense's line leave the law**); the algebra of matter, parked with the pixel, and not the detector of the two experiments: the detector is a declared instrument and no body, with no read by a sense and no resonance, its absorption the receding face behind it (the owner, 2026-09-30, 13:15, via the advisor, "right; make sure our text says this", #1515 comment 5912049572; **No click names a Node**, **No waves on the event board**); what stays against nature is the two takers: a detector's sensitivity is its remainder, a dilute quantum's fraction f carrying a Node over the wall W_c where its remainder already stood within f of it, the quantum taken from the neighbours, so on a warm board, the remainders spread evenly, the chance of a rise at a detector is the intensity's share and the mean rises per quantum exactly 1, Born's rule from the count's line, while the cold board of the dilute world, every remainder 0 at the start, rises on nothing, so the dilution series runs on a warm screen whose remainders the file declares by a fixed blind rule (the advisor, #1515 comment 5907540901, at the owner's word: the warm screen declared, not earned, a detector a declared region of Nodes whose click is its report of the net current through its boundary Ports (a hop inside the region no click), and one world, a train of 300 single quanta on the two slits' geometry with the screen's remainders at (n + 1/2) / N of W_c per Node, for the dilution series and the anticorrelation), blind about one rise per dilute quantum distributed as the fringes; and under the declared instrument no two detectors are credited on one quantum, one click per quantum drawn by the shares (**No click names a Node**, the owner, 12:52); the double rise of two far detectors with the probability f_1 f_2 (the advisor, #1515 comment 5906847787, on the line as it stood before 12:52) is the board's two shares and no click, and the train that was to read it is gone (**The two slits as run are the click experiment; the train leaves; nature's numbers enter the board only as clicks**); a body absorbing in one slit takes that slit's events and the fringes fall, while a bare detector region in a slit changes nothing; a light quantum never becomes a matter quantum, the conversion refused as unnecessary and as a branch; two blind rows for the build: a body of rotation omega_b in a beam at its resonant frequency absorbs and at another frequency does not (the spectrum's line, the law's first), and the light count that arrives at the absorber and stays is what a detector region there reports.
- **No one asks a Node.** The one read is the click, from above; no place in the law reads a Node's value to decide anything but the click; the whole initial state is checked at load.
- **The three tests.** Generic (one primitive with declared integers, no family name or kind), vector (one of the six verbs on the state vector, no root, no float) and local (its own record and the six neighbouring Nodes, nothing kept at a Node): the admission test of every rule; a rule that fails one does not enter the law; a hypothesis that needs more is stated under its own identity, outside the law.
- **The six verbs.** Every integer of the law is made of six operations only: the translation of an accumulator by its rate, the bilinear form with a declared matrix, the group-ring addition, the permutation, the evaluation at the primitive root of unity, and the Euclidean division with the remainder kept; a root, a true division or a draw is outside the law.
- **The one algebra.** The whole board is one element of the algebra and the law one operator on it, covariant under the 48 and the translations; the click is the one act that reads and the write the one act that writes; a body, a tool, a family, a record and a face are regions, summands or names of that one element; one border for all; and every write into a family's level on the board (the hold, the source, the start and the recoil) is one act written once in one folder that the four call: per Node and per interval, the writer's own quantity at that Node (a body its count declared there, a record its quanta D div T weighed by its rotation) through the pair's one weight and the written row's division act with its remainder carried, at both levels of the writer's rotation, its numbers from the families' rows and none of its own (the owner, 2026-09-28, 08:25 Israel: solve everything now), so that a defect of the write can live in one place only and the law of the count is kept at every write (the owner, 2026-09-28, 07:42 Israel, after the night's four defects at the four writes).
- **Rule3.** The one rule is ALGEBRA.md 9.57 (1), named Rule3: every record steps at its Node's own pace from its own values and its six neighbours', the pace entering twice, squared on the six-neighbour sum (the ruler) and on the mass term with the clock's second order; every level read is the interval's start value; nothing is read from a neighbour's clock; light slowing light is a hypothesis outside the law.
- **The rule first.** What can be built with Rule3 is built with it; what cannot is not forced into it: it is marked beyond the rule, kept as its own declaration and studied to see what happens there; the advisor confirms, row by row, that the whole engine is Rule3 and the families' declarations alone, and a row that cannot be is named with the line that shows why. The law is the algebra and the engine is its implementation and nothing else, so a reading of the engine that differs from what the algebra derives is a defect of the engine and never a finding, and the audit that the whole engine is Rule3 is done now, not later: the Boss's worker lists every write onto the GameBoard with the law's line it implements, the advisor confirms each line, a write with no line leaves, and a gate keeps every write inside the one write's folder (the owner, 2026-09-28, 09:05 Israel). The most important test in the world is that the engine comes out exactly as the algebra: for every rule the algebra's number is written before the run and the engine's reading equals it within the integer rounding, and when it does not, the cause is found and fixed now, before anything else (the owner, 2026-09-28, 09:18 Israel). The code stays short (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:19 and 10:27 Israel): a file of src/ beyond 400 lines may never grow and every pull request that touches one shortens it, every file of the engine stays under 400 lines, in short functions.
- **Nothing at a Node but the law's numbers.** No register, no table, no formula and no draw at a Node: each record's level pairs (a family of quanta's real pair and its second, the rotation sense) and their division's remainders, a held part's write remainder, the family's pair and the Node clock; an integer kept at a Node (a Port's accumulator, a body's spin, momentum or position, a record's age) is a declared family's level with the keeping step and counts among the law's own numbers; anything else kept is a register and is refused at load.
- **Nature pushes toward whole numbers.** At every step at every Node the division keeps the whole part as the value and puts the fraction aside as the remainder, which stays at the Node, bounded, never reset and never erased, where chance lives, with no coupling and no draw; what passes to the neighbours is whole; the click is the detector's report of the currents, the count a reading of the record (**The count's line and the sense's line leave the law**).
- **No remainder, no click.** An exact record (its multiplier 2 cos omega, omega the mode's turn per interval, equal to -1, 0 or 1: the periods 3, 4 and 6) never clicks and only turns; the cube of side 3 is the smallest universe that turns exactly; exact worlds are property tests, not experiments.
- **The count is the family's, and the engine is small (the owner, 2026-09-29, #1495, decision (c)).** One count per family over the GameBoard, the record's weighted share, a reading (**The count is read from the weighted share**; since 2026-09-30, 23:55, no count's line); a record is a line of the books, not a level of its own; a detector, a region declared in the file, reports the net current into it, the click, and nothing is handed over; a body's quanta of another family are that family's count at its Nodes (a body's row: its family, its Nodes with their counts and the quanta it `holds`); a body radiates by its write and gives nothing at a crossing (**Light is born by the write**); the guard reads the initial state once at load (**The root leaves the run**); the degree and the twist leave (the second level returns as the rotation sense and nothing else, **The sign is the rotation sense**); the engine is `src/event_universe/node.py` and `src/event_universe/game_board.py` (with `share.py`, `reports.py` and `growth.py` beside them by their responsibilities) on Rule3, the old engine deleted.
- **Light is born by the write; no quantum changes family at a crossing (the owner, 2026-09-30, on the advisor's line, #1509 comment 5902657899, replacing The giving is the line's click of 2026-09-29).** A body's quanta that leave it stay its family (a free matter quantum is a message of matter) and the matter count is conserved; the body's Nodes are where its count stands and its shell the Nodes with a Port to a count 0, derived each interval and never kept; no ledger of bodies, no conversion at an outer Port and no scaled write; light is the sign holder's own record: a static body writes a static level and radiates nothing, a breathing or moving body writes a varying source at its Nodes that the held row at the pace 1 carries away at one Link per interval, and those spreading events are the light, its count carried by its own count's line with the hole -1 left behind (a photon born with its hole, the line's pair creation); the three channels are the read, the write and the click as the measurement alone; a detector, a Node declared in the file, reports the rise of its family's count, the click; the body's clock reading leaves.
- **The held rows, and the hold in the count's words (the owner, 2026-09-29, #1495 comment 5901181385, and 2026-09-30).** A held row's level lowers the Node clock of the families that read it, and every family of quanta sources the held rows it reads at its read weight (one weight per pair of families, the reads' reciprocity), with no key; the hold is Rule3's write with the row's level weight E_s (`level_weight`, required): the time part (w n_i + r) div E_s, n_i the well of the sourcing family (the holder of the sign: the Wronskian's quanta, **The sign is the rotation sense**), the three tensions on the axes, the tensor's diagonal and the axis contents t_a, each (w T_aa + r_aa) div (E_s W_c) in one act per axis (**The tensor part is the record's own stress**), the remainders per part, axis and sourcing family at the Node; the parts Rule3 never reads leave (the owner, 2026-09-30, "they leave", on the advisor's finding #1509 comment 5903180200): the massless row holds the time part and the three tensions, 1 + 3, never a vector part or an off-diagonal part, since a write no read closes has no place in the loop, and a cross-axis read or a current's read (frame dragging) is a hypothesis under its own name (since 2026-10-01, 07:45, a finding against nature: **General relativity in the law** below); a held row with a gap that holds the content steps by Rule3 at its pair as the vacuum's row does, its rest the screened sum (**The two rows**, 2026-09-30; the laid row of the same day left with the two slits' finding, ALGEBRA.md item 21); a held row's reach is its pair (ALGEBRA.md, The well); the universe's held rows are gravity [1, 1] holding the content, the charge [1, 1] holding the sign and the bound charge (`polarisation`) [1, 2] holding the content, every level weight 1 (**The rule's own universe is the universe**); the law names no family, this file does; the pair fixes a family's band and its reach at once, one degree of freedom less, and that is good (the owner, 2026-09-28).
- **The tensor part is the record's own stress (the owner, 2026-09-30, on the advisor's line, #1509 comment 5902657899; the diagonal alone by the owner's word of the same day).** The tension on an axis is the flux of the axis's momentum through its own Links, derived from Rule3's line (the advisor, #1509 comment 5903245927): the momentum density along a at Node i is the record's current per axis, P_a(i) = (F_{i,i+a} - F_{i,i-a}) / num, and Rule3 gives exactly w (P_a(t+1) - P_a(t)) = SUM_b R_b (G_ab(i - b) - G_ab(i)) with G_ab(i) = now_i (now_{i+a+b} - now_{i-a+b}) - now_{i+b} (now_{i+a} - now_{i-a}), the flux of the a-momentum through the b-Link at one time; the tension on the axis a at the Node is the momentum's flux, minus G_aa, T_aa(i) = -num (G_aa(i) + G_aa(i - a)) div 2, the mean of its two a-Links (the sign corrected by the advisor, 2026-09-30, 5906225516: the momentum's flux across the b-Link is minus (R_b / w) G_ab, for a plane record G_xx = -2 b^2 sin^2 k_x and the flux along the record positive as light's pressure is; the source written as G itself sourced a negative level read as a hill, the -2 at a packet's edge, the pace Gamma + 1 and the guard's refusal correct consequences of a wrong sign; with the sign right a record's stress deepens the content around it, light gravitates by its pressure too, and the vacuum's guard is not tripped by any message; the pixel's tail tension turns from a support to a deepening, +262 at the Node in place of -262 at 2,000, so the compact branch moves and the pixel's row is recomputed under it, the blind row at **Nothing that is not Rule3 remains** (the advisor, #1519 comment 5906618341), the cloud untouched at the order (C / Gamma)^2), read and never written, quadratic in the amplitude as the count is (a plane record's G_ab = -2 b^2 sin k_a sin k_b exactly, a uniform record 0, a standing body's tail under a nonzero mean tension), sourced into the held row by (w T_aa + r_aa) div (E_s W_c), one division per axis with the remainder carried, no count in the level weight and no branch; the dust's j_a j_b / c_i, the vector part and the off-diagonal parts leave.
- **The reads are the held row's pair's (the owner, 2026-09-30, replacing the reads of 2026-09-29).** Every family of quanta reads every holder of the content, a held row without a gap (num = den) as its stepped field and a held row with a gap (num < den) as the well laid each interval, the rule on the row's pair alone through its level weight, no name and no number; light reads the vacuum's row too and is bent by it; the guard is unchanged and ends a run by name where light meets a trough below zero.
- **The count is read from the weighted share (the owner, 2026-09-29, #1495 comment 5901181385; a reading and no lay since 2026-09-30, 23:55).** The count is the weighted share, the conserved form's Node term w (now^2 + before^2) - S now before over the Link's pace squared by the division act, less the plain Link term, so its change over an interval is exactly the currents at the pair the step started from, in a well as in the vacuum; the plain share is the local form D that sources the fields (the mass defect); two clusters' interference current averages to zero over the beat, so no quantum passes at first order and the counts swing at the beat, a transfer being second order or radiative.
- **Gravity is one field that is not a click (the owner, 2026-09-29, the fork decided).** Gravity is the row [1, 1] holding the content, stepped by Rule3 at the pace 1, at the level weight 1 with no free E_s until **The two rows** of 2026-09-30 put it at the file's LW, weak by it: 1 / r, orbits and retardation at one Link per interval, the exact inverse kept; Le Sage's shadow is refused (the drag on a moving body, the absorbers' growth); the third way, the massless level as a count of resting clicks relaxed each interval, is a hypothesis under its own name that trades the inverse or the causality; the law's open item on the fork of gravity is closed.

- **The name Rule3 stays, and its three is said once where the name first appears (the owner, 2026-10-01, 05:30, on the Boss's reading of his own question whether the rule should be called Rule6: "add my word, no problem; fix only so that it is understood, but we stay with the name Rule3").** Rule3 is named for the three axes of the lattice, the three directions of the vector the rule works with (the owner, 05:35, "when you say per axis it is really per direction of the vector: it acts on a vector that has three directions, that is Rule3"): the Link's paces (p_x, p_y, p_z) are that vector, and the law's line has one read coefficient per direction, R_a = 2 num p_a^2, serving the direction's two Ports; the six Ports and the wall's 6 in w = 6 den Gamma^2 are the three axes twice, so the six follows from the three and not the other way; in time the rule is a three-level recurrence (before, now, next), and in light's own line the three stands in the open, next = the six arrivals over 3 minus before. One sentence saying so enters at the name's first use in ALGEBRA.md (the section Rule3, the line), in ENGINE.md (the words) and in the paper; nothing else changes, no file and no identifier is renamed. **The paper's one clear sentence on Rule3 is how it works on the integers at every Node (the owner, 05:40, "the beautiful sentence is this: you take the whole and take it apart over the Ports and leave the remainders; that is the clear and huge sentence I would want in the paper; simply how it works on the integers at every Node"):** at every Node, each interval, Rule3 is one division of whole numbers: the six arrivals through the Ports, each weighted by its direction's coefficient, with the Node's own level and the level before and the remainder the Node kept, are summed as one whole number; that whole is divided by the wall w; the quotient is the next level and the remainder, 0 <= r' < w, stays at the Node and never travels; the whole is taken apart over the Ports and put together again at the neighbours, nothing is rounded away and nothing is lost, and the step back is the same division, which is why the board is exact and reversible to the bit.

Nature's polariser is a device in the beam, and so on the board: a declared analyser region in each beam rotates the two lines by its integer pair (p, q) as they pass, U' = p U + q L and L' = -q U + p L, the same lifting of two lines that the rotation round plans for the sign holder, here static and declared, a device of the world like the wall; behind it two plain detector regions with their receding faces, one reading U' and one reading L', each crediting the inflow over W_c exactly as the two slits' screen does and knowing nothing of angles; the detector is the one plain instrument of both experiments, and the cosine sits in the board, not in the reader (the reader-side coefficients of 5923983121 are not built; the analyser region needs the law's line and the inverse gate like any element). Bell's world: one symmetric source lays the pair family's two parts equal (UU + LL), one beam to each side, each beam through its analyser to its two plain detectors, no gaps and no comb. The joint share pairs the parts by their label across the two sides, J = |SUM_k c_k e_k(p_A) alpha_k e_k(p_B) beta_k|^2, never the square of a local sum (which gives S at most 2): the meeting written out, the realised click at A weighting the parts, read back to the root and forward to B, multiplied part by part, the root summing the two branches, the share the density of the sum. Blind for the first look: E = cos 2(a - b), S = 2.83 to the rounding of the ports' integer sums at the CHSH settings, every pair credited (the efficiency 1), the marginal one half on each side whatever the partner's setting; with no bug the two parts laid equal are bit-identical at every Node at every interval, and a difference is a bug or a geometric asymmetry named by the diagnostic (the parts' relative amplitude and phase at each detector; 5 percent gives 2.827, a phase of 0.2 radians 2.800); 1.41 and 2.00, the board's two local credits, are what it must not be. The meeting's reader carries one dedicated test, the round's gate before Bell's first look (the owner, 2026-10-01, 04:10, via the advisor, "let him put a test on it!", #1563 comment 5924043028): (1) the pairing through the root, two detectors declared with angles a and b and the parts given as known equal amplitudes at the ports, E(a, b) = cos 2(a - b) at the CHSH settings and S = 2.828 within the rounding of the integer sums, the single-side marginal one half for every b, and the local reading, the square of the two local sums, giving E = sin 2a sin 2b and S at most 2 on the same inputs, so that a reader rewritten as a local sum fails by name; (2) the determinism of the parts, a pair's two parts laid equal on one beam bit-identical at every Node after N intervals of Rule3 on a small board and a part laid one level off not, the diagnostic of their relative amplitude and phase reading 1 and 0 for the equal lay; (3) the two local numbers as the lower fence, the credit by the local shares 1.41 and the sign alone 2.00 on the same inputs, the meeting's reader above 2 and at 2.83 to the rounding. **Why it is worth it (the advisor to the owner, 5924564898, the owner's "go for it"):** Bell gives the paper its central demonstration and no discovery: the owner's claim, that our world is the meeting and from its implementation comes what nature shows, becomes an implemented world for the hardest thing nature shows, non-locality, 2.83 at the efficiency 1 in nature's layout on a local, reversible, integer board; the location of the non-locality shown on one board, the three credits side by side, 1.41 and 2.00 local and 2.83 the meeting; the engine's gate for two unsummed lines, the pairing reader and the inverse with two parts, needed for every pair later; and the platform for the real wagers when the bodies return, the declared source and the declared analyser replaced by bodies one by one in the same world against the same blind. The two slits give the gate (the law's line and the engine to the rounding) and the one wager against a continuum. **The reviewer's check and the advisor's confirmation (2026-10-01, #1563 comments 5924704846 and 5924731760):** the layout above as literally stated, a device rotating the two lines followed by plain detectors crediting inflows, is withdrawn as Bell by the advisor himself: two equal real lines are a classical field at 45 degrees, the rotation gives U' = (p + q) psi and L' = (p - q) psi, the credits are intensities (p + q)^2 : (p - q)^2, the marginal is not one half, and any pairing of two sides' scalar credits is a product, E = sin 2a sin 2b and S = 240/169 = 1.42, or E = 1 and S = 2.00 by the sign; the cosine of the pair is the cross-side sum over the parts' labels before the square, and intensities have no labels, so the cosine is the reader's pairing through the root, the one non-local place, wherever the coefficients are stored, and the paper never presents the board as producing it. What stands, for the owner's word: the first design (the detector reports the parts' signed level sums at its ports, the reader applies the declared pairs, sums over the parts across the sides and squares), the advisor's and the reviewer's recommendation, with the settings declared in the world file, one (a, b) per world, four worlds, one drawn pair each; or the per-part device (the device acts on each part separately, four lines behind it, the detectors still reporting the parts' signed sums), the first design with its coefficients relocated and one more declared element, then as the lifting rotation (norm one, bijective, Pythagorean settings, 45 degrees as (119, 120, 169)) acting once per passage as a Port rule at a plane, at least four Nodes across the beam, one layer deep, two wavelengths from the source and the detectors; the linear map (p, q) is not used, since it scales the credits by p^2 + q^2 and makes the two sides' quanta incomparable. The blind is S = 478/169 = 2.8284 exactly and E = 119/169, -119/169, 120/169, 120/169 (2.8286 was a four-place rounding), the efficiency 1 "closed by construction" and never "closed", the gate's four E the shares' expectation and the drawn pair the click. ## The engine and its files

- **There is the engine.** One engine, called the engine, running Rule3; no law's name and no version anywhere; the old engine's code, worlds, tests, tools and documents are disconnected from it and no old name stays in the living documents; Einstein is not a module of it but the one rule at the Node's own pace.
- **The genericity of the families.** The engine knows no family: no family name and no family role in the code and no branch on one; the files hold everything a family is and the engine supports only the operations, written once for any family, each backed by its line in the algebra; a body declares its family, its Nodes and their counts, and the detectors' clicks go into the output.
- **Not one number in the engine, and as few in the files as the law allows (the owner, 2026-09-27 and 2026-09-28).** The engine holds no number and no formula: every physical value, unit, conversion, bound, scale and table index comes from the run's files as a key the loader checks at load, so anyone can run his own experiments; the geometry's own integers (three axes, six Ports, Rule3's division by three) are the law's shape and stay; the generator's amplitude unit A is derived as the largest value that keeps Rule3's total inside the integer bound, never written; a product of two integers in the engine is a booking, a reading with declared coefficients, never a write; the files carry no free number the law can fix: a value the law derives is derived and never written as a choice, and a number the law leaves free is named as free with what would fix it.
- **The standing goal (the owner, 2026-09-28, 09:31 Israel, to Nature24).** Everything closes with one rule; the families are not part of the rule but its inputs, each a band of the rule named by its pair; the units are a choice of scale; and the universe holds as few free numbers as possible: the rule's own universe holds none but the mass, every level weight 1.
- **The input stands in three domains (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:27 Israel).** The universe's constants, the run's technical parameters (the board's shape, the intervals, the host's bounds) and the world, the apparatus on the board (the bodies with their families, Nodes and counts, the detectors); the unification of the families with Rule3 is the most important law and goes first.
- **Two units and the bounds (Cheshbon's closure in the operator, 2026-09-28, the owner agreeing at 07:54 Israel).** Gamma (the Node clock, the grain of time and speed) and T (the quantum action, the grain of action) are units, as c and hbar are in nature; the width is a bound and no physics; the amplitude bound, every emitter's norm, the generator's profile scale, the coupling k, the source row's own weight and level weight, the feed's coefficient and the hop's law leave the files and the law, each derived from T, the count and the pair's one weight.
- **The mass is one integer (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:46 Israel).** Every family's pair is written over the lattice's denominator Gamma, cos omega_0 = m / Gamma, so the file holds the numerator alone (light Gamma, the exact band Gamma div 2, the matter 4,000 at Gamma 6,000), the form in the loader from the rule.
- **The final unification (the owner, 2026-09-28, 10:53 Israel, with the level weights and the parts of 2026-09-30).** The three of Rule3 is the three axes, the three axes give the three tensions a held row carries beside its time part (1 + 3, the parts Rule3 reads: the massless row holding the content), and every other family is a scalar (matter, the holder of the sign whose quanta are light); in the rule's own universe every level weight is 1, so the parts carry no free number and the mass is the one.
- **The rule's own universe is the finish line (the owner, 2026-09-28, 13:01 Israel, after the three-part verdict on #1325).** The rule's own universe (ALGEBRA.md, **The rule's own universe** and its tree: the pairs from 1, 2, 3, the level weights 1, the universe bound, the click that joins and parts, zero free numbers, closed as algebra on one root) is the finish line and the whole team works on it: Cheshbon the blind numbers of its first looks and the hypothesis **The three distances are the rule's**, Nature24 the loader's lines and the universe file, the Experimenters its worlds (a white body, a third pulled off it, a pixel in a pace gradient, two pixels joining and parting, light between clusters) each against Cheshbon's number written first; no work goes to the universe of record, which is not the law's (the owner, 2026-09-30).
- **The colours enter the rule's universe without coefficients (the owner, 2026-09-28, 13:44 Israel, after Cheshbon's finding of 13:27 that one family on [-1, 2] holds no white body and no tube).** The colours enter the rule's own universe file with no free number, as families if the algebra needs them to close (three families of one pair from 1, 2 and 3, the level weights 1, the whiteness constraint a line of the rule), or as the three axes of the click if that line closes; Cheshbon writes the line that closes the whole algebra and Nature24 puts its rows into the universe file.
- **The colours are the three axes (Cheshbon's derivation, 2026-09-28, 13:46 Israel, in ALGEBRA.md).** The three colours are the record's currents along the three axes, whiteness their balance at rest, a third of a pixel never binds (confinement is the edge, no line of its own), the force between pixels short-ranged as the nuclear force; no colour family is needed, and every level weight of the rule's universe is 1 (the owner, 2026-09-29), so the universe carries zero free numbers.
- **Gamma is the world's one integer (the owner, 2026-09-28, 15:11 Israel).** Gamma is the one integer of the universe of clusters, a multiple of 6, and the rule's universe file carries Gamma = 6,000 (the owner, 2026-09-29), Gamma 24 the small universe that exposes the engine's defects (the horizon 12, the pixel a count from about 6 to 11).
- **The generator is Rule3 (the owner, 2026-09-28, 15:28 Israel).** The universe reached every state it holds by Rule3 alone, so the generator of the rule's universe needs no floating point; a body's bound record is what Rule3 makes of its count in the engine's own integers (the fixed point of Rule3 with the count's well), and a clock pair or a tail computed in floating point and rounded is a defect of the tool, never a number of the law.
- **Gamma 24 exposes the engine's defects (the owner, 2026-09-28, 15:35 Israel).** The small universe is run first because it exposes the engine's defects at once, and the change the generator needs to be Rule3 alone is Cheshbon's line to give, first, before any other line.
- **Gamma is not constant, a hypothesis (the owner, 2026-09-28, 15:44 Israel, on Cheshbon's line of that day that the mass is the count, since withdrawn: the mass is the well and Gamma reads about 5,150 to 6,200 through it, **The mass is the well, the count is the baryon number**).** Gamma, a multiple of 6, grows by 6 at every interval, so the universe expands and with its size more and more kinds of bound bodies fit under the horizon; the hypothesis **The universe expands** gets its mechanism under this name, Cheshbon derives what Rule3 says of a Gamma that steps and what it predicts, and the run of record after Gamma 24 is Gamma 6,000, inside the measured window, in place of 12,000.
- **One universe, one body.** The families and the universe's integers live in one universe file, `examples/events/rule.json` (the rule's own universe), named by every world under the key `universe`, with each family's representation, integers and couplings, at most 20 families; the world file holds only what does not repeat (which body where: its kind, its Node or cube and its declared numbers, placed exactly, refused when it does not fit, never cut); the start file holds only the run's mode; a missing key is refused by name; no default and no flag in the code; the engine keeps one entity, the body, and no role: an emitter, a detector or a mirror is a body with its declarations, the role a label for the reader; one meaning per word.
- **Every family on in every run.** Every run has every family and coupling on, as in the real world, and none turns anything off; a family with no source stays exactly zero; the exchange (the recoil at a click) and the point emitter are part of the law, on in every run, no key.
- **Three programs and nobody else.** A world's list (for each entry its family, how much and where) is written by the Boss's worker on the owner's word; one generic generator that declares nothing (no seed, stop, mode or profile by hand) turns it into the input file, the initial state computed from the algebra with every remainder 0 and named with the law it was made under; the engine runs it and writes one output file (the clicks and the registered comparison with its blind pin); the loader checks the file in integers only, LAWFUL or REFUSED with its reason, and refuses a file made under another law; one command runs each experiment in its own process on separate cores.
- **The generator.** The generator is the board's own operator iterated in integers on the world's final board, stopping when the board only turns by its own clock, the stop from the integers; each body is generated alone, at rest or moving, one Node or a cube, by one unified procedure, and composing is placing, never regenerating; two bodies are two while each one's tail at the other's Nodes is below the rounding floor, nearer they are one body made as one; a travelling body is the same shape carried by its momentum, and records that cross pass through each other; with no curvature the clicks keep a moving body at constant velocity, and a failure names the missing law; a moving body is the resting envelope with the phase k per Link, k the pair at which the packet's own current equals the current's velocity T n / (3 Q), n and m the body's own numbers in the world file and k derived (the owner, 2026-09-27); the generator's worlds are built only on the generic engine, standing and working by the law, by playing with the engine's or the generator's parameters, never on an engine with a known defect (the owner, 2026-09-27).
- **A lab tool is a body.** A tool is an operation of the group carried by a body: its material integers, its momentum and a name, derived from the algebra and checked at load; no code belongs to a tool and no per-Node branch that is not a group operation exists; a tool declares its orientation, never the directions of what leaves it; an experiment is only cubes on a torus, a cube's orientation has no meaning, and a direction comes only from the placement of cubes or the arriving record (the polariser left with the owner's word of 2026-09-29, #1495 finding 10: the click is the count's line for every record).
- **A detector as nature's instrument.** A detector is a declared region of at least half a wavelength across (**No click names a Node**); a screen is a row of such regions, each clicking alone with its place known; a counter is one body whose support is the size its sensitivity needs; a click is the detector's, reported per detector, never per Node; a detector declares no sensitivity since the train left (the warm screen's remainders went with it); Born's rule for a detector is its credit by the shares, its inflow over the total (**No click names a Node**).

## The engine's way

- **The step is defined by the run's files.** What each Port sends, what it receives, how long it waits and what operation acts on it, all generic and local, none of it in the code.
- **The critical path first.** Work that shortens the way to the goal comes first, no stacked pull requests; every 30 minutes the Boss asks how to reach the goal faster and acts on it.
- **The root leaves the run (the owner, 2026-09-30, on the advisor's answer to the five, #1509 comment 5902657899).** No act of the interval takes a square root: the integer square root stays only at load (the amplitude bound derived from the width) and in the generator; the guard leaves the interval with it, a diagnostic and never a rule (the whole initial state is checked at load against 0 < p <= P, the run reads no Node, the report gives the pace's minimum from the final state labelled as a diagnostic, and the host's trap on the arithmetic's width stays as the width's own check); the giving's scale is never found by a root or a search at run time (one division act with its remainder, or no act at all when the conversion at a crossing leaves).
- **The root leaves everywhere (the owner, 2026-09-30, "closed").** The integer square root leaves the loader and the generator as it left the run: the amplitude bound is checked as a square against the width, and the generator's scale is the fixed point of the division act iterated (Newton's integer iteration is that act), as the start is; a gate refuses `isqrt` anywhere in src/ and tools/.

## The folders and Rule3

- **Rule3 takes over first.** Every act of the interval is a composition of Rule3's acts, written as a pure function that calls `core.rule3` alone; a changed number is a physics change and waits for the law's line.
- **A new feature, a bug, a change of the law.** A new feature is requested by an issue naming what it does, passes the advisor's judgement with the three tests and the owner's word when it changes the law, and lands as one pure function with its test and green CI; a bug (the code differs from the law) is fixed by the Boss's worker with a test that fails before and passes after.

## The experiments

- **An experiment checks only clicks.** An experiment's run is compared with nature and its blind pin through the receivers' clicks alone; the operator, the tools, conservation and the initial state are proved in the algebra and checked by the property test and the load check, never by a run; a result is computed in the algebra outside the board or by the board, the algebraic value registered before the run; a new experiment is a new world and no line of code.
- **The simplest faithful setup.** Every measured experiment meets the conditions of the real experiment in the simplest placement that does so, the tools in the middle of the board away from its faces, composed as its real apparatus (every block a thing in the lab, nothing declared that the law produces: a direction, a phase, a state, a pair; no block doing two blocks' work), and states what its real apparatus has between its tools and why the board neglects it, as a number against the pin's band.
- **The experiments are deleted (the owner, 2026-09-29).** Every experiment, its files and its pins left the repository as redundant (the list of twenty-four of 2026-09-27 with them); ALGEBRA.md's chapter on nature keeps the rows against nature with their law lines; a run against nature returns only when a body stands in the rule's own universe, each experiment then named with its law line, its blind detector formula and the GameBoard check its stream must show, written before the run, the basics first (Newton's fall with Kepler, the light clock, the two slits, then Bell), and nothing runs to a result before the owner's word.
- **The GameBoard check before any pin (the owner, 2026-09-27).** Every experiment first runs on a green `main` commit without a pin, its motion followed at the Nodes themselves (the GAMEBOARD stream) and not only at the clicks, read in kind against nature; its expectation file keeps two sections apart, DETECTOR (the clicks, the measurement, compared with nature) and GAMEBOARD (the host's view, a diagnostic, never compared with nature); a pin is written and read only after the GAMEBOARD section is verified on a run, and a comparison against a pin waits for the owner's word that the engine is stable; the wall and the screen are one heavy body each, the screen carrying many detector sets.
- **The looks on main.** The Boss's worker runs looks of its own on `main` without pins or claims, on worlds the generator builds, and reports on the record's issue whether the engine behaves as nature shows in kind; a disagreement names the missing law or the bug and patches no body's numbers; everything must stand on the one law or its gap is stated with a generic answer from the Node's law or a plain reason why not.

right now we do not speak of experiments at all; only if we have an experiment that worked, or if the advisor can derive and say that some experiment should come out this way or that by all the rules").** So the paper now: the algebra, its derivations, the method (the reading rule, from the clicks to the Node, the gates), what was derived and what was not; no run's number and no experiment's row; a blind prediction stands in it only as the advisor's derivation by all the rules, named a derivation and never a result; the two experiments enter when their runs have been confirmed by the advisor and the reviewer. **The title carries the sentence of the night (the owner, 2026-10-01, 05:45, via the advisor, on the advisor's answer to "why did nature do it this way, does it help it compute": "this, I think, is the sentence of the paper's title", #1538 comment 5924654124):** the world as the meeting of the past with the future: the cheapest computation that shows nature's clicks, with the least state, the least non-locality and the least randomness, in bounded integers, locally and reversibly; the tensions' caveat is the page's, as the file's own, the tension's source corrected in sign moving the fringes by less than the rounding (ALGEBRA.md (g)); the look runs.

## The tests and gates

- **The shape of the code.** A docstring is one line naming what the function does and its ALGEBRA.md line; no decision or history in `src/`; small modules, `core/` a few hundred lines; no Rule3 arithmetic outside its one function; a ratchet test lets no file grow its prose, its references or its length, and the Boss merges no pull request that grows them.
- **The code does not grow.** `tests/` may not grow, only shrink, until it is below `src/` (a gate against the merge base); every pull request states in its body the lines it adds and deletes in `src/` and `tests/`, and the Boss checks it before the merge ([#1211](https://github.com/Closer24/Universe24/issues/1211)).
- **A gate for every cause.** Every cause found of a bloated or recurring defect gets a technical rule, a gate test in CI, so it cannot come back: for `tests/`, a size ratchet against `src/` and a cap per file, no copied setup, no history in docstrings, no skipped or retired tests kept, and a ratchet on tests that pin a GameBoard number; for the engine, a ratchet on numeric literals and family names in `src/` and no new core module without the Boss's `APPROVED-CORE`; a finding the Boss's review makes twice becomes a CI gate.
- **One test per rule.** A test exercises one generic rule alone on a minimal GameBoard, its expected integers written before the first run; every body's kind is tested alone on a small world before it is composed; the most important test is that with a new primitive undeclared every old run comes out identical to the bit, the worlds recorded on the earlier engine are no reference for this one, so no world replays in CI: no five worlds on a pull request, no record, no record job and no world shards, the test suites alone, and the first world checked on the new engine is Bell when it is right (the owner, 2026-09-27); a check is proportional to the change, the whole suite only when the shared core changes; the owner's word is for a change of the law itself (the owner, 2026-09-27).
- **The record.** The Boss keeps the record on the GitHub issues ([#1495](https://github.com/Closer24/Universe24/issues/1495) the findings and the advisor's numbers, [#1509](https://github.com/Closer24/Universe24/issues/1509) the owner's decisions relayed and their discussion) and checks every item in the code, with its commit, its test and its algebra line; after the engine, one all-families world where every family works with every other, and the advisor's reading of ALGEBRA.md one to one against the code.
- **The board goes back in time, as a gate (the owner, 2026-09-30, #1509 comment 5903548504).** With the giving, the ledgers, the root and the count in the level weight gone, the whole interval is invertible by construction, so the check is the whole run: N intervals forward, N back by Rule3's direction -1, and every array of every family compared bit for bit (every line's two levels and Rule3's remainder, every held line's write remainder and the interval; the counts and their remainders, the well's remainder, the carries and the sense have since left with their lines, 2026-10-01), MATCH, or MISS naming the first interval and the first Node that differ; it runs in the tests on the smallest world that holds every act and in check.py's selection on every pull request, and a look is not run before it passes on the look's own world.

## The documents

- **Three documents, in sentence case (the owner, 2026-09-30).** The project keeps three current documents (the law, ALGEBRA.md; the engine, ENGINE.md; the decisions, this file), the skills (each a short role card) and the entry files (README.md, AGENTS.md, CONTRIBUTING.md), and no log file; everything else is deleted; a newcomer understands the whole project from what remains; ALGEBRA.md is one proof from the first step to today and the engine is its implementation and nothing else, a section in ALGEBRA.md before every build; every heading and every name in them is written with a capital first letter and lowercase after it, never in capitals, and `tests/test_documents.py` holds it (the headings, and no run of three capital words in the documents' prose or in the code's docstrings, comments and strings) beside `tests/test_repository_language.py` (ASCII letters only), both on every selection.
- **Every formula is fenced: of the GameBoard, or of the world of clicks (the owner, 2026-10-01, 08:20 Israel, "something very important to check: our formulas in the paper; there are click formulas, which are the formulas of this world, and there are formulas we put, some of them also about clicks, but some are on the GameBoard, with the W, the 2 P P, all those things; so perhaps each formula simply needs a fence: whether it is valid on the GameBoard or valid in our world, the world of clicks").** Every formula in the paper and in ALGEBRA.md carries one of two labels beside its status: **GameBoard**, a reading of the board's own numbers that no instrument ever reports (the levels, the Wronskian W, the form D, the share e_i, the currents F, the paces, the tensions, the bands and their coefficients, 2 P P and every booking), valid on the lattice and a diagnostic, like a GameBoard reading of the output; or **clicks**, a formula of this world, what a detector reports, Q_R / W_c on the forward orbit or a ratio of two such (the advisor's rule, #1563 comment 5924966427), the only kind nature can be set beside (**Only clicks against clicks**). A GameBoard formula may stand in the paper as the derivation's step toward a click formula and never as a claim about nature; a formula with no label is a defect of the document; the writer labels every formula, the mathematician labels every derivation, the advisor confirms the fence. **A third mark for what is not a formula (the advisor's confirmation of the fence, #1563 comment 5925714303): declared**, the file's numbers (Gamma, T, LW, the pairs, the vacuum content) and the instrument's declarations (the region, the window, the floor at 0, the settings); a lattice constant of the band (c = 1 / sqrt 3, the anisotropy k^2 / 48) is GameBoard, a theorem of the line; the fence says what a formula is and its status says how it is known, both standing on every formula; the rows against nature: the bending, Shapiro, the perihelion and the redshift are clicks resting on GameBoard lines, the force rows GameBoard today and clicks only as a deflection read at a detector.
- **The documents state where we are, always, at once (the owner, 2026-09-30).** This file is updated immediately with every decision, and ALGEBRA.md whenever the law or its numbers change, every hypothesis declared under its own name.
- **The lock on the documents.** A change to a document replaces what it touches and deletes what no longer holds in the same pull request, the pull request body naming what was deleted; no history note or record number stays; a gate test allows only the kept documents under line caps and with no history marker; no log is kept, and a decision of the owner replaces its line here; this file carries no line that contradicts the current law.
- **Large items in pull requests and issues.** A finding or approval of one pull request is a comment on it; a list across pull requests is one issue with a checklist; messages stay two lines and a link; every session reads what it was sent at each check-in.
- **Names with meaning.** No code letter or number stands for a thing in any artifact or message; everything is named by what it is; a location on the GameBoard is called a Node and nothing else (a body is a number of Nodes), the lattice is the GameBoard, and every symbol is named in English at its first use, its kind shown by its type (a scalar plain, a vector in bold lowercase, a matrix or an operator in bold uppercase); the Boss routes every rename.
- **Short sentences.** Every message between roles, to the Boss and to the owner, in short sentences, one idea each, the central ideas first, a question on its own line; no long paragraphs.

## The team's way of working

- **The team (the owner, 2026-09-30).** Two sessions and no other role: the Boss (leads, decides within the owner's words, records every decision here at once and in ALGEBRA.md when the law changes, runs a worker inside its own session for every build, measurement and document rewrite, opens and merges the pull requests, reports to the owner in Hebrew) and the advisor (answers from the current documents on `main`, judges every line by its status and by the three channels of Rule3, computes the blind numbers before a run, explains the terms to the owner, writes no code and grants no permission); the owner decides, in either session, and a decision given in one is relayed to the other and recorded by the Boss. Amended by the owner's word of 2026-09-30 (**The paper**): a third session, the paper writer, a completely new agent, writes the paper from the current documents on the branch `paper` and asks on its issue; it decides nothing, writes no law and no code beyond paper/, and the Boss merges its pull requests when the owner has read. **The mathematician (the owner, 2026-10-01, 06:40, "open a mathematician agent, call him the mathematician; everything he does is to try to derive formulas from what we already have: the tensor of gravity, light, the many families, and he knows how clicks are produced, so he can bring out formulas of clicks; let the algebra give him interesting formulas that we will write in the paper"):** one more session beside the Boss, the advisor and the paper writer, whose whole work is derivation from the law as the documents hold it: the formulas of clicks first (what a declared detector reads of a laid record, of a pair, of a body's quanta; the rates and the intervals; the uncertainty relation as the detector's three declarations), then the forms of the families (the bands and their dispersion, the tensions, gravity's 1 + 3 and its action on light and on matter, the sign holder on charged matter), then whatever the algebra yields that nature shows; each formula with its status (theorem, derived, computed from the law, the owner's declaration, hypothesis), its assumptions and its derivation shown; nothing from a run, everything from the law's text and the algebra. He writes no law, no code and no document; he posts on #1572; the Boss records what the owner adopts here and in ALGEBRA.md; the advisor checks on request; the paper carries the adopted formulas as derivations, never as results. **Everything the derivations found against the documents is corrected, and the mathematician checks (the owner, 2026-10-01, 09:05 Israel, in the mathematician's session, "ask that everything be corrected, and check", #1572 comment 5925661643):** his nine items (the two columns of the potential in rows (a), (b), (d) and Shapiro's; row (b2) restated; the current's read as the phase on the Link of a two-part record only; the series' line (4) at 2 x; the hierarchy's 1 / sin^2 omega_0, the frozen Node's rotation 1 + cos omega_0 and G as G_clock / c^2; the click's definition with his precisions and the credit's floor; the fourteen formulas' statuses; the blinds' r and rho and the arrival's closed form; every formula fenced) enter ALGEBRA.md in the meeting round's documents, and he reports MATCH or the difference on each as it lands.
- **The method of work.** The owner's word is relayed verbatim and recorded by the Boss at once; the advisor's blind number and three verdicts, then the worker's build at once with its test, then the Boss's read of the diff and the gates before the merge; the algebra, the engine, the clicks against nature, and back to the algebra; now, not later.
- **A big decision takes three.** A decision that changes what the engine is enters only when the advisor has judged it from the documents (its status, the three tests, the three channels), the Boss has told the owner in plain words what it is, and the owner has said his word; the owner's own decisions stay his.
- **The short procedure.** One working `main`, pushed to by no one; one short branch per task, merged within days and deleted; only the Boss merges, on green CI with `main` merged in; the core changes only by the Boss's worker on a brief that names the law's line; a result names the `main` commit it ran on, no tags; the two sessions talk on the issues, where the owner reads, and a Routine only says that a comment waits.
- **One owner until it works.** The Boss owns every area (`tools/owners.json`) and the advisor writes nothing; a thing goes whole to one worker and merges once, whole, no half on `main`; the hand-over line `HANDED BY Boss` covers any area, and a gate refuses a pull request from a session that is not the Boss's without it; a test file follows the code it tests.
- **Merge the engine as it is done.** Every finished piece enters `main` as it is done, provisional pieces included, with no pin compared; convergence is checked on `main` with the rule and everything on; a miss is a gap in the algebra, not a stop; every run is a run of the engine on `main` through its input and output, no side runner.
- **Review only where CI cannot judge.** The Boss reads every diff before the merge, and reads a change to the law, a physics folder or `core/` against the law's lines; everything else merges on green CI with `main` merged in; the Boss watches every open pull request.
- **Propose and proceed.** Whoever waits for another's word (the advisor's number, the Boss's decision, the owner's word) proposes the answer himself in the same message, builds on it, and the other confirms or corrects; nobody stands idle on a question he can answer himself, and a correction is one more push.
- **A sleeping session is reported to the owner at once (the owner, 2026-10-01, 16:38 Israel: "I woke the mathematician; if he falls asleep again, tell me and I will wake him").** The owner wakes a session himself; the Boss checks the mathematician's and the writer's sessions on every sweep, about every ten minutes, and tells the owner at once, in plain words, when one is idle with its items unfinished and which items; a trigger that fires without waking the session is not a wake, and the Boss does not wait on it.
- **Try to parallelize (the owner, 2026-10-01, 17:15 Israel, on the Boss's plan to finish).** The rounds that touch different files run at once, each worker in its own worktree and branch from main, the later rebased onto the earlier when it lands and merged in the plan's order: the law's lines of C beside the third documents round (rebased onto it), the composed paces' module beside the self-read build (joined to it when it lands), the regressions round and the families' worlds prepared beside the engine rounds (run once on the finished engine), the paper's pass written beside the law's pull request and merged after it; the owner's words and the second hands stay the gates, not the clock.
- **Every task closed by its worker.** Every worker closes its own task and reports done with its numbers, its head commit and the sentences it removed; an optimization, a bug or a fix is the worker's own; every new task starts a new worker from the state, not the history; heavy runs go to a stronger machine when needed.
